[EVENT: TUR28495]
Date: 2023-01-01
Country: Turkey | Location: Mus
Type: Strategic developments > Change to group/activity | Disorder: Strategic developments
Actor1: Government of Turkey (2016-) [State forces]
Fatalities: 0
Sources: Cumhuriyet; Haber 7; Kurdistan24 [National-International]

DESCRIPTION:
Security measures: On 1 January 2023, Mus Governor's office issued a ban on all forms of marches, protests, press conferences, sit-ins and other political gatherings in the province for 14 days in order to maintain public order and prevent 'provocative events'. Similar measures have been imposed in the province (and several other southeastern provinces) for some years to maintain public order, presumably against political events that can be organized by Kurdish movements.

---

[EVENT: TUR28847]
Date: 2023-02-06
Country: Turkey | Location: Kahramanmaras
Type: Strategic developments > Change to group/activity | Disorder: Strategic developments
Actor1: Government of Turkey (2016-) [State forces]
Fatalities: 0
Sources: Beyaz Gazete; Haber 7; Haberler; Ihlas News Agency; Kurdistan24 [National-International]

DESCRIPTION:
Security measures: On 6 February 2023, the Turkish Government announced a series of measures in 10 provinces (Kahramanmaras, Adana, Malatya, Adiyaman, Hatay, Sanliurfa, Diyarbakir, Gaziantep, Kilis and Osmaniye) that were directly affected by two major earthquakes that claimed tens of thousands of lives. The measures included the suspension of school education in all 10 provinces for 2 weeks, the suspension of university education in all 10 provinces until further notice, shutting down daycare facilities for one week, suspension of civil aviation in Adana, Gaziantep and Kahramanmaras airports until further notice. On 7 February, an entry ban was imposed for civilian vehicles in Hatay, Kahramanmaras and Adiyaman provinces, effective for 48 hours. The epicenter of earthquakes (Kahramanmaras) coded as location.
